Latest finding: UNION preparation amplification fixed, with generated SQL excerpts and measurements ↓

SQLite case study: c0_minimal_0

Local artifact. Captured 2026-09-22 from sprefa-wt/sqlite-perf-main using the current sqlite_ivm release extension. The graph is clickable. Click a node to load its source path, evidence status, timing, and code excerpt.

Case fact

232 closure rows cost 8.66 s for this direct evaluation. The input contains 231 seeds. The expected closure contains exactly one row outside the input seed set, the kernel nil sentinel. The actual output also contains 232 rows and 0 diagnostics. The user fixture has 12 rules and the source input fixture has 0 runtime facts that create a new user fact in this case.

Measured call and phase graph

Click a node

Node colors: observedsource pathsource-level suspectouter loop not exercised

First three suspects

1. View installed before nonlinear rounds

measured declare_view runs at src/_6_eval/_7_sqlite_eval.rs:360-369, then run_rounds at line 374. The declaration costs 2,867.8 ms. The first changed round costs 5,629.7 ms for 82 rows. Parent trace evidence identifies the hot shape as an INSERT OR IGNORE into a material kernel table from another ordinary material table, which enters the already-installed incremental view.

Next experiment: bootstrap material tables to fixpoint before installing the incremental program view, then compare closure and a later delta. No implementation change is made in this report.

2. Per-set/program maintenance cost

partial measurement The direct run executes 28 round statements. One writes 82 rows. The other 27 return zero rows and sum to about 3.4 ms. The residual candidate is the changed round plus the view's per-node maintenance and live reads. The current capture does not expose an exclusive hafley-observe SQLite profile split.

The graph keeps repeated no-op calls visible without treating their count as the hot cost.

3. Cache guard rebuild amplification

unmeasured in c0 rules_fingerprint and the seed containment check select fresh_engine for changed rules or retractions. This can amplify declaration and round work in an outer compiler run. The isolated direct c0 command does not exercise a repeated rebuild.

Measure with a repeated outer case before changing cache behavior.

Call sequence and loop boundary

direct case measured:
  dl8 eval
    → Evaluate::reduce (SQLite branch)
      → evaluate_sqlite
        → SqliteEvaluate::connect
        → SqliteEvaluate::declare
          → declare_tables + flush rows + write seed delta
          → CREATE VIRTUAL TABLE "program" USING sqlite_ivm(...)
          → run_rounds
            → 28 nonlinear_round execute calls
        → read_view + read_seeds
        → closure rows / minted terms

outer compiler context, not exercised by this command:
  Rounds::reduce
    → round_closure
    → Evaluate::reduce / evaluate_sqlite
    → answer_round + assemble_generated_program
    → repeat until stable

The dashed graph node is the outer context. Direct dl8 eval starts at the evaluator input and bypasses Rounds::reduce; conclusions about outer repeated rounds require a comptime driver capture.

What the c0 oracle input contains

rules = 12
seeds = 231
trace evaluate_sqlite seeds = 232

The generated declaration contains macrotime/eval lowering relations such as
reader_node/intern_rows and ordinary material tables. Those generated rules
are the program executed by this case. The source fixture's copy rule is not
a literal one-to-one label for every generated SQL CTE.

This distinction keeps the source form separate from the frozen oracle stage program.

Generated SQL under study

declare_view sql produced 4,195 bytes. The excerpt below is the actual captured SQL, with the middle elided. It starts with recursive CTEs and material-table reads, then installs the virtual table named program.

Update: UNION preparation amplification fixed in f8cab4b

The earlier suspects above record the investigation before attribution. A process stack sample then placed 2,891 of 2,922 maintenance samples (98.9%) inside SQLite statement preparation. The sample covers a five-second window beginning two seconds after launch. These are statistical stack counts, not an exact allocation of wall time.

Why the old operator multiplies work

The UNION emitter places the left-input query in both the representative selection and the membership exclusion. Each input contains upstream operator definitions. Repeated references drive SQLite SELECT-tree duplication and compilation through the nested chain. Merely naming the left input with a MATERIALIZED CTE still took 8.68 seconds in a trial and was discarded.

-- Structural excerpt of the OLD emitter; L and R stand for generated input SQL.
SELECT representatives FROM (L)
UNION ALL
SELECT representatives FROM (R)
WHERE key NOT IN (SELECT key FROM (L));  -- L appears a second time

-- Implemented selection: each input appears once.
SELECT c0, ..., c8, 1
FROM (
  SELECT c0, ..., c8,
         row_number() OVER (
           PARTITION BY __k ORDER BY __side, __row_identity
         ) AS __representative
  FROM (
    SELECT c0, ..., c8, __k, rowid AS __row_identity, 0 AS __side FROM (L)
    UNION ALL
    SELECT c0, ..., c8, __k, rowid AS __row_identity, 1 AS __side FROM (R)
  )
)
WHERE __representative = 1;
-- Side 0 wins; within that side the smallest exact row identity wins.

Population source boundary restored

The historical plugin at 1a93386 read named operator input tables. Initial population now reads each child's existing completed scratch output and retains it until its last consumer finishes. Aggregate metadata holds one additional read reference. This adds no persistent per-input row copies. Maintenance continues through the existing live-input and signed-delta paths.

The old plan searched the native dictionary index using only __node=?, then checked touched-ID membership. The changed SQL drives a CROSS JOIN from touched IDs into dictionary integer-primary-key lookups. This also covers stored before-images through the same source helper.

Telemetry limitation discovered during this audit

hafley-observe currently reads cumulative sqlite3_stmt_status counters. Summing profile events can include cached statements' prior population and maintenance executions. The tables above use statements observed exactly once with run=1. The raw receipts retain every observation and explicitly mark this restriction. Disabling the caller connection's cache did not disable the virtual table's separate connection-wrapper cache. No shared telemetry implementation was changed.

Diagnostic command:

cd /Users/chrishafley/projects/sqlite_ivm && cargo test --offline --locked --test 19_source_reads maintenance_source_read_costs -- --ignored --nocapture

79 plugin tests and four rebuilt native-extension tests passed. The explicit diagnostic passed at all three sizes. Summary · Before SQL, plans and counters · After SQL, plans and counters

Counter attribution corrected

hafley-observe now reads and resets SQLite statement counters at each PROFILE callback and clears existing prepared-statement counters when tracing starts. MEMUSED remains a statement memory gauge. Two runs of the same cached statement now report equal per-execution VM steps. Previous cost pins included cumulative cached executions and have been regenerated. The 32/1,024/8,192 UNION diagnostic consisted of single-run statement samples, so the figures above remain valid.

One-row nested UNION at 8,192 source rows: with materialized Set fences 10,437,802 VM steps; a trial allowing Set CTE inlining used 15,172,928 VM steps. The inlining trial was reverted. The report therefore retains the completed-source-table fix for initialization and the original CTE fences during maintenance.

Indexed Set membership after one-key update

The source update and three-level UNION query are identical in both runs. One input key changes.

WITH r0 AS (SELECT k,v FROM source_rows), r1 AS (SELECT k,v FROM r0 UNION SELECT k,v FROM r0), r2 AS (SELECT k,v FROM r1 UNION SELECT k,v FROM r1), r3 AS (SELECT k,v FROM r2 UNION SELECT k,v FROM r2) SELECT k,v FROM r3;
UPDATE source_rows SET v=v+1 WHERE k=1;

Mutual recursion exposed stale scratch rows

Correction to the two preceding milestones: their c2 hashes matched, but the later sqlite_emit_2_mutual gate failed at both commits. The recursive member table was correct. The Odd result table included rows left by an unused Even projection.

starts(0) ── edges(0→1→2→3→4) ──► parity(side,n)
                                      ├── side=0 ─► even(n), unused
                                      └── side=1 ─► odd(n), result

Old population: node 13 even ─► slot 13 ─► zero readers, rows retained
                node 19 odd  ─► slot 13 ─► old rows + odd rows
Fixed:          node 13 even ─► slot 13 ─► zero readers, DELETE
                node 19 odd  ─► slot 13 ─► odd rows

Regression SQL, using source tables starts(n) and edges(a,b):

WITH RECURSIVE parity(side,n) AS (
  SELECT 0,n FROM starts
  UNION SELECT 0,e.b FROM parity p JOIN edges e ON p.n=e.a WHERE p.side=1
  UNION SELECT 1,e.b FROM parity p JOIN edges e ON p.n=e.a WHERE p.side=0
), even(n) AS (SELECT n FROM parity WHERE side=0),
   odd(n) AS (SELECT n FROM parity WHERE side=1)
SELECT n FROM odd;
Resultn values
Plain SQLite SQL1, 3
Plugin at output-slot milestone0, 1, 2, 3, 4
Plugin after cleanup1, 3

c2_partial_3: captured SQLite replay, 2026-09-22

The driver loads the plugin and the captured source database and term arena. It executes the exact emitted CREATE VIRTUAL TABLE and ordered nonlinear-round statements without starting a Sprefa evaluation process. Source SHA-256: 4ebe8a4acc1b2b76e6c8e0d670ba3a16d20db25eca11f07c3060ae1b0e5d6ce7. Term arena SHA-256: 0f3fa28ef93a1986357942730e23f4b9b0f3dfd329e798f922a61e28745790c9. View SQL SHA-256: a45061150caf62141a64a076ae16bf438de29c9ef61a83c85e1f78a0263219b7. Round list SHA-256: 03cb7c194e3ae9a294d755243d195c302c505749145e49d637ab119eadd7d826.

roles asks live_input for each rule input. rule_from embeds that read in the rule's FROM; round_derives stores the resulting statement. The prior live_rows recursively emitted the set's upstream graph even though program_op…x0 already held its current membership. The new read stops at that maintained table and traverses no unused ancestors. Plain joins and aggregates also omit unused row identity and key expressions from intermediate live reads. Join delta handling, fixpoint member tables, and retraction statements retain their existing paths.
